12:"$Sreact.suspense"
13:T4c118,<h2>一、Rust 基础类型</h2>
<p>Rust 是 <strong>静态类型语言</strong>（编译时确定类型），基础类型分为「标量类型」（单个值）和「复合类型」（多个值组合），类型标注可省略（编译器自动推导），但复杂场景必须显式标注。</p>
<h3>1. 标量类型（4 种）</h3>
<h4>（1）整数类型</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：有符号（<code>i</code> 开头）/ 无符号（<code>u</code> 开头），后缀表示位数（8/16/32/64/128/arch）</li>
<li>常用类型：<code>i32</code>（默认有符号整数）、<code>u32</code>（默认无符号整数）、<code>isize/usize</code>（与系统架构一致，用于索引）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> -</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">42</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 有符号32位整数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 无符号32位整数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> c </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;              </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 自动推导为 i32</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> d</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> usize</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 适合作为数组索引（64位系统是u64，32位是u32）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"a={}, b={}, c={}, d={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, a, b, c, d);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）浮点数类型</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：带小数的数值，仅 <code>f32</code>（单精度）和 <code>f64</code>（双精度，默认）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> f32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#212121;--shiki-dark:#BBBBBB">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">14</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 单精度浮点数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> y </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#212121;--shiki-dark:#BBBBBB">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">718</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;          </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 自动推导为 f64（精度更高，推荐使用）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sum </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> y;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"sum={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, sum); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 sum=5.858</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）布尔类型（<code>bool</code>）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：仅两个值 <code>true</code>/<code>false</code>，占 1 字节，<strong>不可隐式转换为整数</strong>（区别于 C/Python）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> is_ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> bool</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> has_error </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 错误：bool 不能转成整数 → error[E0308]: mismatched types</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // let num = is_ok as i32;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, is_ok </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> has_error); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 逻辑与 → false</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, is_ok </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">||</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> has_error); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 逻辑或 → true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（4）字符类型（<code>char</code>）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：Unicode 字符（支持中文、表情），用单引号包裹，占 4 字节</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ch1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> char</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> 'A'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 英文大写字母</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ch2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> '中'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;         </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 中文汉字（支持Unicode）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ch3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> '😀'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;         </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 表情符号（也是Unicode字符）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"ch1={}, ch2={}, ch3={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, ch1, ch2, ch3);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>2. 复合类型（基础 2 种）</h3>
<h4>（1）元组（Tuple）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：<strong>固定长度</strong>、可包含不同类型值的集合，长度编译时确定，不可修改</li>
<li>语法：<code>(值1, 值2, ...)</code>，访问用「点号 + 索引」，或通过「解构赋值」提取</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 定义元组（类型标注可省略）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">bool</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">25</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式1：点号+索引访问（索引从0开始）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"姓名：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 姓名：Alice</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 年龄：25</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式2：解构赋值（推荐，更简洁）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (name, age, is_student) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> person;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}，{}岁，是否学生：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, name, age, is_student);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 空元组（单元值）：表示「无返回值」，函数默认返回 ()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> unit </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> ();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"空元组：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, unit); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 空元组：()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）数组（Array）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心概念：<strong>固定长度</strong>、相同类型值的集合，存储在栈上（区别于动态集合 <code>Vec</code>），长度不可修改</li>
<li>语法：<code>[类型; 长度]</code> 或 <code>[值1, 值2, ...]</code>，访问用「方括号 + 索引」</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式1：显式长度+重复值（创建5个0的i32数组）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"arr1: {:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, arr1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 arr1: [0, 0, 0, 0, 0]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式2：直接罗列值（自动推导类型和长度）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"arr2[2] = {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, arr2[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 arr2[2] = 30</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 数组长度：用 std::mem::size_of_val 或 .len()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"arr2长度：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, arr2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">len</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 4</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"arr2占用字节：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">mem</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">size_of_val</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr2)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 4*4=16字节（i32占4字节）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 错误：数组长度固定，不能新增元素 → error[E0596]: cannot borrow `arr2` as mutable</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // arr2.push(50);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>整数溢出：调试模式（默认）下溢出会 panic，发布模式（<code>--release</code>）下会环绕（不推荐依赖），建议用 <code>checked_add</code> 等安全方法</li>
<li>字符与字符串：<code>char</code> 是单个 Unicode 字符，<code>&#x26;str</code>/<code>String</code> 是字符序列（后续详细讲）</li>
<li>数组 vs 元组：数组是「同类型 + 固定长度」，元组是「异类型 + 固定长度」</li>
</ul>
<h2>二、Rust 流程控制</h2>
<p>Rust 流程控制分为「条件判断」和「循环」，语法简洁且安全（无空指针风险）。</p>
<h3>1. 条件判断（<code>if-else</code>）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心规则：条件表达式必须是 <code>bool</code> 类型（<strong>不可隐式转换</strong>，区别于 C/Python）</li>
<li>语法：支持多分支 <code>if-else if-else</code>，且 <code>if</code> 可作为表达式返回值（类似三元运算符）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 85</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 基础多分支</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 90</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"优秀"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 80</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"良好"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 60</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"及格"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"不及格"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // if 作为表达式（返回值）：适合简单条件赋值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> grade </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 80</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"A"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> score </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">>=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 60</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"B"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"C"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"等级：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, grade); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 等级：A</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>2. 循环（3 种）</h3>
<h4>（1）<code>loop</code>：无限循环（手动终止）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：不确定循环次数时使用，用 <code>break</code> 终止（可带返回值）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> count </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 无限循环，break 返回值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> result </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> loop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        count </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> count </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">            break</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> count </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 终止循环并返回 10</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"result: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, result); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 result: 10</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）<code>while</code>：条件循环</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：确定循环条件时使用，条件为 <code>true</code> 时执行</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"num: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, num); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 3、2、1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）<code>for</code>：迭代循环（最常用）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：遍历迭代器（数组、切片、<code>Vec</code>、<code>Range</code> 等），安全且高效（无越界风险）</li>
<li>语法：<code>for 变量 in 迭代器</code>，常用 <code>0..n</code>（左闭右开区间）生成迭代器</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历 Range（0..5 → 0,1,2,3,4）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"i: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, i);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历数组（推荐：无需索引，避免越界）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"值：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 10、20、30</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历数组并获取索引：用 .iter().enumerate()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (index, val) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">iter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">enumerate</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"索引{}：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, index, val); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 索引0：10、索引1：20...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 反向遍历：用 .rev()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">rev</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"反向i: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, i); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 4、3、2、1、0</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li><code>if</code> 条件必须是 <code>bool</code>：不能写 <code>if 1 { ... }</code>（C 语言允许），需显式判断 <code>if 1 != 0 { ... }</code></li>
<li><code>for</code> 循环优先于 <code>while</code>：遍历集合时，<code>for</code> 无需手动管理索引，避免越界（Rust 不允许数组越界访问，编译时检查）</li>
<li><code>loop</code> 的返回值：只有 <code>loop</code> 支持 <code>break 带返回值</code>，<code>while</code>/<code>for</code> 不支持</li>
</ul>
<h2>三、Rust 函数</h2>
<p>函数是 Rust 代码组织的核心，语法严谨，支持参数、返回值、递归、嵌套定义。</p>
<h3>1. 核心概念</h3>
<ul>
<li>定义关键字：<code>fn</code></li>
<li>语法结构：<code>fn 函数名(参数列表) -> 返回值类型 { 函数体 }</code></li>
<li>关键规则：
<ul>
<li>参数必须显式标注类型（编译器不推导参数类型）</li>
<li>返回值有两种方式：① 函数体最后一行表达式（无分号）；② <code>return</code> 语句（适合提前返回）</li>
<li>无返回值时，返回类型可省略（默认返回空元组 <code>()</code>）</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. 代码示例</h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 1. 无参数、无返回值函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> greet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Hello, Rust!"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 2. 有参数、有返回值函数（返回表达式结果）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 无分号 → 表达式作为返回值（推荐）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 3. 多返回值函数（用元组包装）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> split_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> even </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">%</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> ==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> even {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 提前返回（带分号）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    (num </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 最后一行表达式返回</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 4. 嵌套函数（内部函数，仅外层可见）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> outer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> inner</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"我是嵌套函数"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    inner</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 只能在 outer 内部调用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 5. 递归函数（计算阶乘）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> factorial</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(n</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">==</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">        1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C"> // 基线条件</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">else</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> factorial</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(n </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 递归调用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    greet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 Hello, Rust!</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sum </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"3+5={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, sum); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 8</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (quotient, remainder) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> split_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"7拆分：商={}, 余数={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, quotient, remainder); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 3,1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    outer</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 我是嵌套函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fact </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> factorial</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"5的阶乘：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, fact); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 120</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>参数类型必须标注：<code>fn add(a, b) -> i32 { ... }</code> 会编译错误，需写 <code>a: i32, b: i32</code></li>
<li>返回值表达式无分号：如果写 <code>a + b;</code>，函数会返回 <code>()</code>（空元组），而非 <code>i32</code></li>
<li>嵌套函数作用域：内部函数只能在外部函数体内调用，不能在外部访问</li>
<li>递归必须标注返回类型：编译器无法推导递归函数的返回类型，必须显式标注</li>
</ul>
<h2>四、Rust 所有权（Ownership）</h2>
<p><strong>所有权是 Rust 最核心的特性</strong>，用于管理内存（无需 GC 和手动释放），核心目标是「避免空指针、悬垂引用、数据竞争」。</p>
<h3>1. 核心规则（必须牢记）</h3>
<ol>
<li>每个值在 Rust 中都有一个「所有者」（变量）</li>
<li>同一时间只能有一个所有者（避免数据竞争）</li>
<li>当所有者离开作用域（<code>}</code> 结束），值会被自动销毁（内存释放）</li>
</ol>
<h3>2. 核心概念解析</h3>
<h4>（1）作用域与自动销毁</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// s 进入作用域（所有者是 s）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 可用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // s 离开作用域 → 自动调用 drop 函数释放内存（无需手动 free）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("{}", s); // 错误：s 已被销毁，无法访问</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）移动（Move）：所有权转移</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：当把一个非 Copy 类型（如 <code>String</code>、<code>Vec</code>）赋值给另一个变量时，<strong>原变量会失去所有权（变为无效）</strong>，避免双重释放</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 所有权从 s1 转移到 s2（s1 失效）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确：s2 是所有者</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("{}", s1); // 错误：s1 已失去所有权（value borrowed here after move）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）克隆（Clone）：深拷贝（手动复制数据）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：如果需要保留原变量的所有权，且复制数据（而非转移所有权），用 <code>clone()</code> 方法（深拷贝，堆上数据复制）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">clone</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 深拷贝：s1 和 s2 各自拥有独立数据</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"s1: {}, s2: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s1, s2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确：两者都有效</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（4）Copy 特性：浅拷贝（无需转移所有权）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：标量类型（<code>i32</code>、<code>bool</code>、<code>char</code> 等）和元组（仅包含 Copy 类型）默认实现 <code>Copy</code> 特性，赋值时会「浅拷贝」，原变量仍有效</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> y </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> x; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// i32 是 Copy 类型，浅拷贝（栈上数据复制）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"x: {}, y: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, x, y); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确：x 和 y 都有效</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 元组（包含 Copy 类型）也支持 Copy</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> t1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> t2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> t1;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"t1: {:?}, t2: {:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, t1, t2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. 所有权与函数传参 / 返回值</h3>
<ul>
<li>函数传参：非 Copy 类型会转移所有权到函数参数，函数执行完后参数离开作用域，值被销毁（除非返回）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> take_ownership</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"函数内：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">} </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// s 离开作用域 → 数据被销毁</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> return_ownership</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 返回 s，所有权转移给调用者</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    take_ownership</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// s1 所有权转移到函数 → s1 失效</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("{}", s1); // 错误</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"world"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> return_ownership</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// s2 所有权转移到函数，再转移到 s3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"s3: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s3); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>区分 Move/Copy/Clone：
<ul>
<li>Move：非 Copy 类型赋值 / 传参，原变量失效（默认行为）</li>
<li>Copy：标量类型，浅拷贝，原变量有效（自动实现）</li>
<li>Clone：手动深拷贝，原变量有效（需显式调用 <code>clone()</code>）</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>堆 vs 栈：Copy 类型通常存储在栈上（快速复制），非 Copy 类型（<code>String</code>、<code>Vec</code>）存储在堆上（深拷贝成本高，默认 Move）</li>
<li>避免不必要的 Clone：优先用引用（后续讲）代替 Clone，提升性能</li>
</ul>
<h2>五、Rust 引用（Reference）</h2>
<p>引用是「指向值的指针」，但<strong>不拥有值的所有权</strong>，核心用途是「在不转移所有权的情况下访问值」，避免频繁 Clone。</p>
<h3>1. 核心概念</h3>
<ul>
<li>语法：<code>&#x26;T</code>（不可变引用）、<code>&#x26;mut T</code>（可变引用）</li>
<li>关键规则（ Rust 安全核心）：
<ol>
<li>同一时间，一个值<strong>要么</strong>有多个不可变引用（<code>&#x26;T</code>），<strong>要么</strong>有一个可变引用（<code>&#x26;mut T</code>）（避免数据竞争）</li>
<li>引用必须始终有效（不能指向已销毁的值，避免悬垂引用）</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. 不可变引用（<code>&#x26;T</code>）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心：只读访问值，不允许修改，支持多个引用共存</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> print_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 只读访问，不获取所有权</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> r1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 不可变引用1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> r2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 不可变引用2（允许共存）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    print_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(r1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 传引用，s 仍拥有所有权</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    print_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(r2);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"s: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确：s 未失去所有权</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. 可变引用（<code>&#x26;mut T</code>）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心：可读写访问值，同一时间只能有一个可变引用（且不能有不可变引用）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> modify_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push_str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">", world"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 可变引用允许修改</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
