<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 变量必须是 mut（可变）才能创建可变引用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> r1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 可变引用1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    modify_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(r1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"r1: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, r1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 hello, world</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 错误：同一时间不能有多个可变引用 → error[E0499]: cannot borrow `s` as mutable more than once at a time</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // let r2 = &#x26;mut s;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("r2: {}", r2);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 引用作用域结束后，可创建新的可变引用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    drop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(r1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 手动结束 r1 的作用域（可选，编译器也会自动回收）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> r2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"r2: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, r2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 正确</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>4. 悬垂引用（Dangling Reference）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心：引用指向的 值已被销毁，但引用仍存在 → Rust 编译时禁止这种情况</li>
<li>错误示例（编译不通过）：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> dangling</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"dangling"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 错误：s 离开函数作用域会被销毁，返回的引用会悬垂</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 编译器错误：error[E0106]: missing lifetime specifier（后续生命周期会讲，核心是禁止悬垂）</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>引用必须与所有者生命周期一致：不能返回作用域内临时变量的引用</li>
<li>可变引用的限制：可变引用存在时，不能有任何引用（包括不可变），反之亦然</li>
<li><code>mut</code> 关键字位置：变量本身可变（<code>let mut s</code>）是创建可变引用的前提</li>
<li>引用不影响所有权：函数接收引用时，不会获取所有权，值的生命周期由原所有者决定</li>
</ul>
<h2>六、Rust 数组与切片</h2>
<p>数组（<code>Array</code>）是固定长度的栈上集合，切片（<code>Slice</code>）是数组 /<code>Vec</code> 的「动态视图」（不拥有数据），是 Rust 中处理集合的常用工具。</p>
<h3>1. 数组（Array）：补充进阶用法</h3>
<p>前文已讲基础，这里补充「数组作为函数参数」和「数组与切片的转换」：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 数组作为函数参数（必须标注长度和类型，如 [i32; 3]）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> print_array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(arr</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"数组：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, arr);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    print_array</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(arr); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 数组是 Copy 类型，传参时浅拷贝（栈上数据）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 数组转切片（&#x26;arr → &#x26;[i32]）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 左闭右开区间，取索引1和2 → [20, 30]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"切片：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 [20, 30]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>2. 切片（Slice）：核心特性</h3>
<h4>（1）核心概念</h4>
<ul>
<li>语法：<code>&#x26;[T]</code>（不可变切片）、<code>&#x26;mut [T]</code>（可变切片）</li>
<li>本质：包含「数据指针」和「长度」的结构体（<code>struct Slice&#x3C;'a, T> { ptr: *const T, len: usize }</code>）</li>
<li>特点：
<ul>
<li>不拥有数据，数据所有权属于原数组 /<code>Vec</code></li>
<li>动态长度（编译时无需确定）</li>
<li>安全访问（编译器检查索引不越界）</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<h4>（2）切片的创建与访问</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式1：指定范围（&#x26;arr[start..end]）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 索引1-3 → [2,3,4]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 从索引2到末尾 → [3,4,5]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 从开头到索引2 → [1,2,3]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice4 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 整个数组 → [1,2,3,4,5]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"slice1: {:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"slice1长度：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">len</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 方式2：可变切片（原数组必须是 mut）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr_mut </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice_mut </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr_mut[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    slice_mut[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 修改切片 → 原数组也会变</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"修改后数组：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, arr_mut); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 [10, 200, 30]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 安全访问：用 get 方法（返回 Option，避免越界 panic）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(val) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"slice1[2] = {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 4</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"索引越界"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）切片作为函数参数（最常用）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心优势：函数可接收任意长度的数组 /<code>Vec</code>（无需标注固定长度）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 切片作为参数（&#x26;[i32] 可接收数组、Vec、其他切片）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sum_slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> total </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        total </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    total</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> vec </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> vec!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">6</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// Vec（后续集合会讲）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">9</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"数组求和：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">sum_slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">arr)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 6</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Vec求和：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">sum_slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">vec)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 15</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"切片求和：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">sum_slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(slice)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 24</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>切片不拥有数据：原数组 /<code>Vec</code> 被销毁后，切片也会失效（Rust 编译时检查生命周期）</li>
<li>可变切片修改原数据：切片是视图，修改切片会直接修改原数据</li>
<li>避免切片越界：用 <code>get(index)</code> 代替 <code>slice[index]</code>（越界时返回 <code>None</code> 而非 panic）</li>
<li>数组 vs 切片：数组是固定长度栈上集合，切片是动态长度视图（推荐函数参数用切片）</li>
</ul>
<h2>七、Rust 字符串</h2>
<p>Rust 有两种字符串类型：<code>&#x26;str</code>（字符串切片）和 <code>String</code>（动态字符串），核心区别是「是否拥有数据」和「是否可变」。</p>
<h3>1. 核心概念对比</h3>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>类型</th>
<th>所有权</th>
<th>可变性</th>
<th>存储位置</th>
<th>核心用途</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>&#x26;str</code></td>
<td>无</td>
<td>不可变</td>
<td>栈 / 堆</td>
<td>字符串字面量、函数参数</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>String</code></td>
<td>有</td>
<td>可变</td>
<td>堆</td>
<td>动态创建、修改字符串</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3>2. <code>&#x26;str</code>：字符串切片</h3>
<ul>
<li>语法：用双引号包裹的字面量（如 <code>"hello"</code>），本质是 <code>&#x26;[u8]</code>（UTF-8 编码的字节切片）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "hello, 世界"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 字符串字面量 → 编译时存入二进制文件，运行时在只读内存</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"长度：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">len</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 11（字节数：hello, 占7字节，世界占4字节）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"字符数：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">chars</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">count</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 8（实际字符数）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 截取切片（按字节索引，注意UTF-8编码，避免截断字符）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// "hello"（安全，未截断字符）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"slice1: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 错误：截断中文字符（"世" 占3字节，索引7-9是"世"）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // let slice2 = &#x26;s[7..8]; // 编译通过，但运行时 panic（invalid utf-8 sequence）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. <code>String</code>：动态字符串</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心：在堆上分配内存，支持动态增删改，通过 <code>String::from("xxx")</code> 或 <code>"xxx".to_string()</code> 创建</li>
<li>常用操作示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 创建 String</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 可变 String</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "world"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 拼接字符串（3种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">'!'</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 追加单个字符 → "hello!"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push_str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">", rust"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 追加 &#x26;str → "hello!, rust"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s2; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 拼接两个 String（s 所有权转移给 s3，s 失效）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"s3: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s3); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 "hello!, rustworld"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 格式化拼接（推荐，支持多个变量）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> name </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 25</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> info </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> format!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"姓名：{}, 年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, name, age);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"info: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, info); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 "姓名：Alice, 年龄：25"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 截取子串（安全方式：用 chars() 迭代）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s4 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">" Rust 编程 "</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> trimmed </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">trim</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 去除首尾空格 → "Rust 编程"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> first_char </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">chars</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">nth</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">unwrap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 获取第2个字符（R）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"trimmed: {}, first_char: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, trimmed, first_char);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 替换字符</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> replaced </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">replace</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Rust"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"RUST"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"replaced: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, replaced); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 " RUST 编程 "</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>4. <code>&#x26;str</code> 与 <code>String</code> 的转换</h3>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // String → &#x26;str（自动解引用，无需手动转换）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "hello"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s_slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">s; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 直接取引用</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // &#x26;str → String（3种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "world"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(slice);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> s3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> format!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"s_slice: {}, s1: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s_slice, s1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>UTF-8 编码：Rust 字符串默认是 UTF-8，不支持直接按索引访问字符（如 <code>s[0]</code> 是字节，不是字符），需用 <code>chars()</code> 迭代</li>
<li>字符串拼接的所有权：<code>s + &#x26;s2</code> 中，<code>s</code> 所有权会转移，<code>s2</code> 需传引用（避免所有权转移）</li>
<li>空字符串：<code>String::new()</code>（空 String）、<code>""</code>（空 &#x26;str）</li>
<li>避免字符串截断：截取切片时，必须确保索引落在 UTF-8 字符边界上（否则 panic），推荐用 <code>chars().nth(index)</code></li>
</ul>
<h2>八、Rust 结构体（Struct）</h2>
<p>结构体是 Rust 中自定义复合类型的核心，用于封装多个相关字段，支持方法定义、关联函数等面向对象特性。</p>
<h3>1. 结构体的 3 种类型</h3>
<h4>（1）普通结构体（最常用）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：包含命名字段，字段可是任意类型</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 定义普通结构体（首字母大写，符合 Rust 规范）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 字符串（拥有所有权）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,        </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 无符号整数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    is_student</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> bool</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 布尔值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 创建结构体实例（字段顺序可任意，需指定字段名）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> alice </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 25</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        is_student</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> false</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问字段（.字段名）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"姓名：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, alice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">name);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, alice</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 修改字段（结构体实例必须是 mut）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> bob </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 22</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        is_student</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> true</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    bob</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 23</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 修改年龄</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob修改后年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, bob</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 结构体更新语法（复用已有实例的字段）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> charlie </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Person</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70"> "Charlie"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        ..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">bob </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 复用 bob 的 age 和 is_student 字段</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Charlie年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, charlie</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 23</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）元组结构体（Tuple Struct）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：无字段名，仅字段类型，适合简单复合类型（如坐标、颜色）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 定义元组结构体</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 坐标（x, y）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Color</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">u8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">u8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">u8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// RGB颜色（r, g, b）</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> p </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> red </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Color</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问字段（点号+索引）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"x: {}, y: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"红色：({}, {}, {})"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, red</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, red</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, red</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 解构赋值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(x, y) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> p;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"解构后 x: {}, y: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, x, y);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）单元结构体（Unit Struct）</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：无字段，类似空元组 <code>()</code>，用于标记类型（如实现特质时）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 定义单元结构体</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> EmptyStruct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> es </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> EmptyStruct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"单元结构体：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, es); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 EmptyStruct（需手动实现 Debug 特质）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 为单元结构体实现 Debug 特质（才能用 {:?} 打印）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#[derive(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> EmptyStructDebug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> esd </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> EmptyStructDebug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"单元结构体（Debug）：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, esd); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 EmptyStructDebug</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>2. 结构体方法（Method）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心：在 <code>impl</code> 块中定义，第一个参数必须是 <code>&#x26;self</code>（不可变引用）、<code>&#x26;mut self</code>（可变引用）或 <code>self</code>（所有权转移）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#[derive(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Rectangle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    width</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 为 Rectangle 实现方法（impl 块）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Rectangle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 不可变方法（只读访问 self）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> area</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">width </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">height</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 可变方法（修改 self，需 &#x26;mut self）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> scale</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> self, factor</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> f32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">width </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">width </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> f32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> factor) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">height </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">height </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> f32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> factor) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 关联函数（无 self，类似静态方法，用 :: 调用）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> square</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(size</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Self { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// Self 是 Rectangle 的别名</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            width</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> size,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> size,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> rect </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Rectangle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { width</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"面积：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, rect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">area</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 调用方法：rect.area()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> rect2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Rectangle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { width</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, height</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    rect2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">scale</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#212121;--shiki-dark:#BBBBBB">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"缩放后：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, rect2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 Rectangle { width: 10, height: 10 }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 调用关联函数（创建正方形）</span></span>
