<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> square </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Rectangle</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">square</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">8</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"正方形：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, square); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 Rectangle { width: 8, height: 8 }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>结构体字段所有权：字段可拥有所有权（如 <code>String</code>）或引用（如 <code>&#x26;str</code>，需生命周期标注）</li>
<li><code>impl</code> 块：一个结构体可有多个 <code>impl</code> 块，拆分代码更清晰</li>
<li>方法参数 <code>self</code>：
<ul>
<li><code>&#x26;self</code>：只读，不转移所有权（最常用）</li>
<li><code>&#x26;mut self</code>：可修改，不转移所有权</li>
<li><code>self</code>：转移所有权（仅当方法需要消费结构体时使用）</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>派生特质：用 <code>#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]</code> 可自动实现常见特质（如 <code>Debug</code> 用于打印，<code>Clone</code> 用于复制）</li>
</ul>
<h2>九、Rust 模式匹配（Pattern Matching）</h2>
<p>模式匹配是 Rust 中强大的控制流工具，通过 <code>match</code>、<code>if let</code>、<code>while let</code> 等语法，可简洁、安全地处理多种情况（如枚举、结构体、Option 等）。</p>
<h3>1. 核心概念</h3>
<ul>
<li>模式：描述数据的结构（如 <code>Some(x)</code>、<code>Person { name, .. }</code>）</li>
<li>匹配：将数据与模式对比，匹配成功则执行对应分支</li>
<li>关键特性：<strong>穷尽性检查</strong>（编译器确保覆盖所有可能情况）、<strong>解构赋值</strong>（提取数据）</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. <code>match</code> 表达式（最强大）</h3>
<h4>（1）匹配枚举（后续枚举会讲，这里用 <code>Option</code> 示例）</h4>
<p><code>Option&#x3C;T></code> 是 Rust 用于表示「可能有值或无值」的枚举，定义为：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">enum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Option</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 有值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 无值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> get_value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Option</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">>) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // match 穷尽匹配 Option 的所有情况（Some 和 None）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> num {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(x) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 匹配 Some，提取 x</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,        </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 匹配 None，返回默认值</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Option</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"a 的两倍：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get_value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(a)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 10</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"b 的默认值：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get_value</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(b)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 0</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）匹配结构体（解构字段）</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#[derive(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> match_point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(p</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> p {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 匹配 x=0，忽略 y（用 _ 通配符）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> _ } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"在 y 轴上"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 匹配 y=0，提取 x（用 @ 绑定变量）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"在 x 轴上，x={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, x),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 匹配 x=y，绑定为 val</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"x=y={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 匹配所有其他情况（用 .. 忽略剩余字段）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"其他坐标：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, p),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 在 y 轴上</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 在 x 轴上，x=3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 x=y=4</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> }); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 其他坐标：Point { x: 2, y: 3 }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）匹配数值与通配符</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> match_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> num {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">        1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"一"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">        2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> |</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"二或三"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 多模式匹配（用 | 分隔）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">        4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">9</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"四到九"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 范围匹配（..= 包含终点）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        _ </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"其他数字"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 通配符（匹配所有未覆盖的情况，避免穷尽性错误）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 一</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 二或三</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 四到九</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    match_num</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 其他数字</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. <code>if let</code>：简化单分支匹配</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：当只关心一种匹配情况，其他情况忽略时，替代 <code>match</code>（更简洁）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> opt </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 等价于 match opt { Some(x) => println!("x={}", x), _ => {} }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(x) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> opt {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"x={}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, x); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 x=3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> point </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, y</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> };</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 匹配结构体的单个情况</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    if</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Point</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> { x</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> } </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> point {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"在 y 轴上"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 在 y 轴上</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>4. <code>while let</code>：循环匹配</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：当循环中只关心一种匹配情况时，持续执行直到不匹配</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> stack </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> vec!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 持续弹出栈顶元素，直到栈为空（None）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    while</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(val) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> stack</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">pop</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"弹出：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 3、2、1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>穷尽性检查：<code>match</code> 必须覆盖所有可能情况，否则编译错误（如 <code>Option</code> 必须匹配 <code>Some</code> 和 <code>None</code>）</li>
<li>通配符 <code>_</code>：用于忽略不需要的字段或值，避免穷尽性错误</li>
<li>绑定变量：用 <code>@</code> 可将匹配的部分绑定为变量（如 <code>x @ 1..=10</code>）</li>
<li>多模式匹配：用 <code>|</code> 分隔多个模式（如 <code>2 | 3</code> 匹配 2 或 3）</li>
</ul>
<h2>十、Rust 常见集合</h2>
<p>Rust 标准库提供了多种常用集合（存储在堆上，动态长度），核心包括 <code>Vec&#x3C;T></code>（动态数组）、<code>HashMap&#x3C;K, V></code>（键值对）、<code>HashSet&#x3C;T></code>（无序集合）等。</p>
<h3>1. <code>Vec&#x3C;T></code>：动态数组（最常用）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心特性：动态长度、随机访问、尾部增删高效</li>
<li>语法：<code>Vec::new()</code> 创建空 Vec，<code>vec![]</code> 宏快速创建</li>
<li>常用操作示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 创建 Vec（3种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> v1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Vec</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Vec</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> v2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> vec!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 宏创建，自动推导类型</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> v3 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> vec!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 创建 5 个 0 的 Vec → [0,0,0,0,0]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 尾部添加元素</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    v1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    v1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">push</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"v1: {:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, v1); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 [4,5]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问元素（2种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"v2[0]: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, v2[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 索引访问（越界 panic）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> v2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// get 方法（越界返回 None，安全）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(val) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"v2[1]: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"索引越界"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历 Vec</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">v2 {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 1、2、3</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 可变遍历（修改元素）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> v2 {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 每个元素乘 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"v2 乘 2 后：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, v2); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 [2,4,6]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 插入、删除元素</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    v2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 在索引1插入 3 → [2,3,4,6]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    v2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">remove</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 删除索引3的元素 → [2,3,4]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"v2 插入删除后：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, v2);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 转换为切片</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> slice </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">v2[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">..</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"切片：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, slice); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 [3,4]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>2. <code>HashMap&#x3C;K, V></code>：键值对集合</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心特性：无序、键唯一、基于哈希表实现，需从 <code>std::collections</code> 导入</li>
<li>常用操作示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 必须导入 HashMap</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">collections</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">HashMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 创建 HashMap（2种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> map2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashMap</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">([(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"a"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">), (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"b"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)]); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// Rust 1.56+ 支持</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 插入键值对（若键已存在，会覆盖值）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">25</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">22</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">26</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 覆盖 Alice 的年龄为 26</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问值（2种方式）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice 的年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, map1[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 索引访问（键不存在 panic）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) { </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// get 方法（键不存在返回 None）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Some</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(age) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob 的年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, age), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 22</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        None</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> =></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob 不存在"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历 HashMap（无序）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> (key, value) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">map1 {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, key, value); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 Alice:26、Bob:22（顺序不确定）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 插入时检查键是否存在（避免覆盖）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">entry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Charlie"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">or_insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">23</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 若 Charlie 不存在，插入 23</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">entry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">or_insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 若 Alice 存在，不修改</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"map1 插入后：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, map1);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 更新值（基于旧值）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> map1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">entry</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">or_insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// Bob 的年龄加 1 → 23</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob 更新后年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, map1[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. <code>HashSet&#x3C;T></code>：无序集合（无重复元素）</h3>
<ul>
<li>核心特性：无序、元素唯一、基于哈希表，需从 <code>std::collections</code> 导入</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">collections</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> set </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 自动去重 → {1,2,3}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 插入元素（重复元素会被忽略）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">insert</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 重复，无效果</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 检查元素是否存在</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"是否包含 3：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">contains</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 遍历集合（无序）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> val </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, val); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 1、2、3、4（顺序不确定）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 集合操作（交集、并集、差集）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> set2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> intersection</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;_> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">intersection</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set2)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">collect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 交集 {3,4}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> union:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;_> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">union</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set2)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">collect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 并集 {1,2,3,4,5}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> difference</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> HashSet</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;_> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> set</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">difference</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">set2)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">collect</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 差集 {1,2}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"交集：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, intersection);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"并集：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">union</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"差集：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, difference);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>集合的可变性：所有集合的修改操作（<code>push</code>、<code>insert</code>、<code>remove</code>）都需要 <code>mut</code> 关键字</li>
<li><code>HashMap</code> 的键要求：键必须实现 <code>Eq</code> 和 <code>Hash</code> 特质（标量类型、<code>String</code> 等默认实现）</li>
<li>遍历顺序：<code>HashMap</code> 和 <code>HashSet</code> 是无序的，若需要有序，用 <code>BTreeMap</code>/<code>BTreeSet</code></li>
<li>内存管理：集合存储在堆上，元素被自动销毁（所有权由集合拥有）</li>
</ul>
<h2>十一、Rust 返回值与错误处理</h2>
<p>Rust 的错误处理哲学是「显式错误」，避免隐式异常（如 Java 的 <code>Exception</code>），核心通过 <code>Result&#x3C;T, E></code> 处理可恢复错误，<code>panic!</code> 处理不可恢复错误。</p>
<h3>1. 不可恢复错误：<code>panic!</code></h3>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：处理逻辑错误（如数组越界、断言失败），触发后程序终止并打印调用栈</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 手动触发 panic</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // panic!("程序异常终止");</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> arr </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">];</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 数组越界 → 自动 panic（调试模式下打印详细调用栈）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("{}", arr[5]);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<ul>
<li>注意：发布模式（<code>cargo build --release</code>）下，<code>panic!</code> 会优化调用栈打印，仅输出错误信息</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. 可恢复错误：<code>Result&#x3C;T, E></code></h3>
<p><code>Result&#x3C;T, E></code> 是 Rust 用于表示「成功返回 T 类型」或「失败返回 E 类型」的枚举，定义为：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">enum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">E</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">T</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 成功</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    Err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">E</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 失败</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（1）返回 <code>Result</code> 并匹配处理</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">File</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 读取文件：成功返回 File，失败返回 io::Error</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> read_file</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">File</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    File</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">open</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// File::open 本身返回 Result&#x3C;File, io::Error></span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> read_file</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"test.txt"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(file) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"文件打开成功：{:?}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, file),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"文件打开失败：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, e), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出错误信息（如 "No such file or directory"）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）<code>?</code> 操作符：简化错误传播</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心用途：当函数返回 <code>Result</code> 时，用 <code>?</code> 自动处理 <code>Err</code>（直接返回错误），仅保留 <code>Ok</code> 的值</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">File</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{self, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Read</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">};</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 读取文件内容：错误自动传播（无需手动 match）</span></span>
