<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> read_file_content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> file </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> File</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">open</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// ? 自动处理 Err：返回错误</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> content </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    file</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">read_to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> content)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// ? 再次传播错误</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(content) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 成功返回 Ok(content)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> read_file_content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"test.txt"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(content) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"文件内容：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, content),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"读取失败：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, e),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）自定义错误类型</h4>
<ul>
<li>核心：通过枚举定义自定义错误类型，实现 <code>std::error::Error</code> 特质（用于统一错误处理）</li>
<li>示例：</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">fmt;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 自定义错误类型</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">#[derive(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Debug</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">)]</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">enum</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    FileError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 包装 IO 错误</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    ParseError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">),           </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 解析错误</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 实现 fmt::Display（错误信息格式化）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Display</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self, f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;mut</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Formatter</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> fmt</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">FileError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> write!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(f, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"文件错误：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, e),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ParseError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(s) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> write!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(f, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"解析错误：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, s),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 实现 Error 特质（标记为错误类型）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 包装 IO 错误（从 io::Error 转换为 MyError）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> From</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> std</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">io</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">Error</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">FileError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 读取并解析文件（返回自定义错误）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> parse_file</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Result</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> content </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> read_file_content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(path)</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">?</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// ? 自动将 io::Error 转换为 MyError（因 From 实现）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    content</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">parse</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">map_err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">_</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">|</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> MyError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">ParseError</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"无法转换为整数"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()))</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    match</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> parse_file</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"number.txt"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Ok</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(num) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"解析结果：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, num),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Err</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(e) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"错误：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, e),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li><code>?</code> 的使用限制：只能在返回 <code>Result</code> 或 <code>Option</code> 的函数中使用</li>
<li>错误转换：用 <code>From</code> 特质可自动将一种错误转换为另一种（如 <code>io::Error</code> → 自定义错误）</li>
<li>错误链：用 <code>source()</code> 方法可获取原始错误（需实现 <code>Error</code> 特质）</li>
<li>优先用 <code>Result</code> 而非 <code>panic!</code>：除逻辑错误外，尽量返回 <code>Result</code> 让调用者处理错误（更灵活）</li>
</ul>
<h2>十二、Rust 模块化</h2>
<p>Rust 的模块化系统用于组织代码（拆分文件、控制访问权限），核心是 <code>mod</code>（定义模块）、<code>pub</code>（暴露公共接口）、<code>use</code>（导入模块 / 成员）。</p>
<h3>1. 核心概念</h3>
<ul>
<li>模块（Module）：代码的逻辑分组（如 <code>user</code> 模块、<code>utils</code> 模块）</li>
<li>包（Crate）：Rust 的编译单元（可执行程序或库）</li>
<li>路径（Path）：访问模块成员的方式（如 <code>crate::user::User</code>、<code>std::fs::File</code>）</li>
<li>访问控制：默认私有（模块内可见），用 <code>pub</code> 标记为公共（外部可访问）</li>
</ul>
<h3>2. 单文件模块</h3>
<h4>（1）定义模块与访问控制</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 定义模块（默认私有）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 私有函数（仅 utils 模块内可见）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> private_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"utils::private_func"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 公共函数（外部可访问）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> public_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"utils::public_func"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        private_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 模块内可访问私有函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 嵌套模块</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> math {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 定义公共模块（直接暴露给外部）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> user {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 公共结构体</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 公共字段</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,         </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 私有字段（仅 user 模块内可见）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 公共构造函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">                age,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">        // 公共方法（访问私有字段）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">        pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> get_age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问模块成员：路径方式</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">public_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 utils::public_func → utils::private_func</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sum </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"sum: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, sum); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 输出 8</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 访问公共结构体</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Alice"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">25</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"姓名：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">name); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 访问公共字段</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"年龄：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get_age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">()); </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 通过方法访问私有字段</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 错误：无法访问私有成员</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // utils::private_func();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // println!("{}", u.age);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）<code>use</code> 关键字：简化路径</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 导入模块成员，简化访问</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> user</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">;</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 无需写完整路径</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> sum </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"sum: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, sum);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Bob"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">22</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"姓名：{}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">name);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 导入整个模块</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">math;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"math::add(2,3) = {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">    // 别名导入（避免冲突）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">public_func </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">as</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> pub_func;</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    pub_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>3. 多文件模块（实际项目常用）</h3>
<p>当模块代码较多时，可拆分为独立文件，目录结构如下：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="plaintext" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="plaintext" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span>src/</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── main.rs          # 入口文件</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>├── utils/           # utils 模块目录</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>│   ├── mod.rs       # utils 模块入口（必须）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>│   └── math.rs      # utils::math 模块</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span>└── user.rs          # user 模块</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（1）<code>src/user.rs</code>（user 模块）</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 公共结构体</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> String</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">impl</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> &#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">str</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        Self {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> name</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">to_string</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(),</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">            age,</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> get_age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">self) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">        self</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">age</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（2）<code>src/utils/math.rs</code>（utils::math 模块）</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> multiply</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(a</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, b</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-></span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> i32</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    a </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> b</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（3）<code>src/utils/mod.rs</code>（utils 模块入口）</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 私有函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> private_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"utils::private_func"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>

<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 公共函数</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> public_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"utils::public_func"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    private_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 导入 math 模块（公共暴露）</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">pub</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> math; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 对应 math.rs 文件</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h4>（4）<code>src/main.rs</code>（入口文件）</h4>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark"><code data-language="rust" data-theme="min-light min-dark" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 导入多文件模块</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils; </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 对应 utils/mod.rs</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">mod</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> user;  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 对应 user.rs</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#C2C3C5;--shiki-dark:#6B737C">// 简化访问</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">use</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">math</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">{add, multiply};</span></span>

<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">fn</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> main</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    utils</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">public_func</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">();</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"add(3,5) = {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">add</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"multiply(2,4) = {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">multiply</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">,</span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">));</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    </span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    let</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> User</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">::</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">new</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"Charlie"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#1976D2;--shiki-dark:#F8F8F8">30</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">);</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">    println!</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#22863A;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">"{}: {}"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">, u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">name, u</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D32F2F;--shiki-dark:#F97583">.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">get_age</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">());</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-light:#24292EFF;--shiki-dark:#B392F0">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>关键注意事项</h3>
<ul>
<li>模块命名规范：模块名用小写蛇形命名（如 <code>utils_math</code>）</li>
<li><code>pub</code> 的作用范围：<code>pub</code> 仅暴露给父模块，子模块的公共成员需显式标记</li>
<li><code>mod.rs</code> 的作用：目录模块必须有 <code>mod.rs</code>（Rust 2018+ 也支持直接用目录名.rs，但 <code>mod.rs</code> 更通用）</li>
<li>绝对路径 vs 相对路径：
<ul>
<li>绝对路径：从 crate 根开始（如 <code>crate::utils::math::add</code>）</li>
<li>相对路径：从当前模块开始（如 <code>super::parent_func</code>，<code>super</code> 表示父模块）</li>
</ul>
</li>
